```
	public static void GetStatScalingFactors(int numPlayers, out float balance, out float boost)
	{
		balance = 1f;
		boost = 0.35f;
		for (int i = 1; i < numPlayers; i++)
		{
			balance += boost;
			boost += (1f - boost) / 3f;
		}
		if (balance > 8f)
		{
			balance = (balance * 2f + 8f) / 3f;
		}
		if (balance > 1000f)
		{
			balance = 1000f;
		}
	}
```

```
		private bool TrySpawnAnNPC(Player player)
		{
			SetSpawnFlags(player);
			GetSpawnRate(player, out var spawnRate, out var maxSpawns);
			if (player.nearbyActiveNPCs >= (float)maxSpawns)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Main.rand.Next(spawnRate) != 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!FindSpawnTile(player, out var spawnTileX, out var spawnTileY, out var xRange))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (!CheckNotSpawningOnScreen(spawnTileX, spawnTileY))
			{
				return false;
			}
			int spawnTileType = 0;
			int spawnWallType = 0;
			GetProperGroundSpawnTileTypeAndWallType(spawnTileX, spawnTileY, out spawnTileType, out spawnWallType);
			if (!PostCheckChosenSpawnTile(spawnTileX, spawnTileY, spawnTileType, spawnWallType))
			{
				return false;
			}
			SetSpawnFlagsForChosenTile(spawnTileX, spawnTileY, spawnTileType, spawnWallType);
			if (dualDungeonsSpawnRules && ZoneDungeon)
			{
				if (!player.ZoneDungeon)
				{
					ZoneDungeon = false;
				}
				else
				{
					Tile tile = Main.tile[pX, pY];
					if (tile == null || !Main.wallDungeon[tile.wall])
					{
						ZoneDungeon = false;
					}
				}
			}
			SpawnAnNPC(spawnTileX, spawnTileY, spawnTileType, xRange, player.whoAmI);
			if (Main.netMode == 2)
			{
				SyncNewlySpawnedNPCs();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static bool CanSpawnEnemiesNear(Player player)
		{
			if (!player.active || player.dead)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (Main.IsJourneyMode)
			{
				CreativePowers.SpawnRateSliderPerPlayerPower power = CreativePowerManager.Instance.GetPower<CreativePowers.SpawnRateSliderPerPlayerPower>();
				if (power != null && power.GetIsUnlocked() && power.GetShouldDisableSpawnsFor(player.whoAmI))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (player.isNearNPC(398, MoonLordFightingDistance))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}
```

```
		public static void SlimeRainSpawns(Player player)
		{
			int y = Main.MaxWorldViewSize.Y;
			int x = Main.MaxWorldViewSize.X;
			float num = 15f;
			if ((double)player.position.Y > Main.worldSurface * 16.0 + (double)(y / 2) || player.nearbyActiveNPCs > num)
			{
				return;
			}
			float num2 = player.nearbyActiveNPCs / num;
			int num3 = 45 + (int)(450f * num2);
			if (Main.expertMode)
			{
				num3 = (int)((double)num3 * 0.85);
			}
			if (Main.IsJourneyMode)
			{
				CreativePowers.SpawnRateSliderPerPlayerPower power = CreativePowerManager.Instance.GetPower<CreativePowers.SpawnRateSliderPerPlayerPower>();
				if (power != null && power.GetIsUnlocked() && power.GetRemappedSliderValueFor(player.whoAmI, out var value))
				{
					num3 = (int)((float)num3 / value);
				}
			}
			if (Main.rand.Next(num3) != 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			int num4 = (int)(player.Center.X - (float)x);
			int maxValue = num4 + x * 2;
			int minValue = (int)((double)player.Center.Y - (double)y * 1.5);
			int maxValue2 = (int)((double)player.Center.Y - (double)y * 0.75);
			int num5 = Main.rand.Next(num4, maxValue);
			int num6 = Main.rand.Next(minValue, maxValue2);
			num5 /= 16;
			num6 /= 16;
			if (num5 < 10 || num5 > Main.maxTilesX + 10 || (double)num6 < Main.worldSurface * 0.3 || (double)num6 > Main.worldSurface || Collision.SolidTiles(num5 - 3, num5 + 3, num6 - 5, num6 + 2) || Main.wallHouse[Main.tile[num5, num6].wall])
			{
				return;

			if (Main.rand.Next(200) == 0)

					type = -3;

			else if (Main.rand.Next(10) == 0)

				type = -7;

			else if (Main.rand.Next(5) < 2)
			{
				type = -3;
			}
			NewNPC(GetSpawnSourceForNaturalSpawn(), num5 * 16 + 8, num6 * 16, type);
```

```
		public NPC SpawnNPC(int X, int Y, int Type, int Start = 0, float ai0 = 0f, float ai1 = 0f, float ai2 = 0f, float ai3 = 0f, int Target = 255)
		{
			if (NPCID.FromNetId(Type) == 1)
			{
				if (RollLuck(180) == 0)
				{
					Type = -4;
				}
				if (Main.tenthAnniversaryWorld && RollLuck(180) == 0)
				{
					Type = 667;
				}
			}
			if (Target == 255)
			{
				Target = defaultTarget;
			}
			int num = NewNPC(GetSpawnSourceForNaturalSpawn(), X, Y, Type, Start, ai0, ai1, ai2, ai3, Target);
			NPC nPC = Main.npc[num];
			if (Main.dualDungeonsSeed && (double)Y > Main.worldSurface * 16.0 && Y < Main.UnderworldLayer * 16)
			{
				ParticleOrchestrator.BroadcastOrRequestParticleSpawn(ParticleOrchestraType.InScreenDungeonSpawn, new ParticleOrchestraSettings

					PositionInWorld = new Vector2(X, Y),
					MovementVector = new Vector2(nPC.width, nPC.height),
					UniqueInfoPiece = Type
				});
			}
```

````
Important flag semantics (defined at 1046–1050 inside `SetSpawnFlagsForChosenTile`), easy to misread:

- `surfaceSpawn = spawnTileY <= Main.worldSurface`.

### `Main.eclipse` (region 3459–3522) and `Main.bloodMoon`

Eclipse region condition (3459): `((double)spawnTileY <= Main.worldSurface || (Main.remixWorld && (double)spawnTileY > Main.rockLayer)) && Main.dayTime && Main.eclipse`. Pool, in order:
477 Mothron (Plantera), 251 Eyezor, 466 Psycho (Plantera), 463 Nailhead (Plantera), 467 Deadly Sphere (Plantera), 159 Vampire, 253 Eyezor (all-3-mechs), 469 The Possessed, 468 Dr Man Fly (Plantera), 460 Butcher (Plantera), 162 Frankenstein, 461 Creature from the Deep, 462 Fritz, fallback 166 Swamp Thing.

Blood-moon gates inside `SpawnAnNPC`: 3532 (`!Main.bloodMoon` required for living-tree gnome), 4476 (HM only Clown 109), 4523/4528 (`RollOnlyBadLuck(300)==0 && (bloodMoon || ZoneGraveyard)` → 53 The Groom / 536 The Bride), 4543 (`bloodMoon && 1/5 < 2` → 489 Blood Zombie / 490 Drippler). Note 489/490 also spawn in graveyards regardless of moon.

### Pre-hardmode enemies displaced (not removed) by hardmode

Vanilla never deletes a pre-HM entry — it inserts HM branches earlier in the same `else if` chain so they win probabilistically. True near-replacements:
- **Demon 62 → Red Devil 156** (Underworld, 4/5 after any mech) — 4799
- **Hellbat 60 → Lava Bat 151** (Underworld, 4/5 after any mech) — 4812
- **Giant Worm 10 → Digger 95** (dirt layer 2/3; cavern via 4863/4871) — 4721, 4863
- **Devourer 7 → World Feeder 98** (Corruption surface, 3/4) — 3615
- **Wall Creeper 164 → Black Recluse 163** (spider nests, 9/10) — 1580
- **Piranha 58 → Angler Fish 102**, **Blue Jellyfish 63 → Green Jellyfish 103** (water, 2/3) — 1845, 1900
- **Pre-HM desert pool (69/580/581/537/508/509) → HM pool (524–533/510)** at 4/5 — 1637
- **Ice Bat 150 → Ice Elemental 169**, **Hornet family → Moss Hornet 176**, **cavern Skeleton set → HM armor set (77/110/197/206)** — 5128, 3742, 4959
- Dungeon: pre-Plantera dungeon (29 Dark Caster, 31/32/34/70/71/72/294–296) is *not* removed post-Plantera; the `hardDungeon` pool is tried first at 2/3 probability (2620).

## B. Hardmode stat-scaling mechanism (exact spec)

Call sites: `ScaleStats(...)` at **8322** (inside `SetDefaults_FromNetId` path) and **17791** (end of `SetDefaults`), always
`ScaleStats(spawnparams.playerCountForMultiplayerDifficultyOverride, spawnparams.difficultyOverride)`.

`NPCSpawnParams` (`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria/NPCSpawnParams.cs`): `float? sizeScaleOverride`, `int? playerCountForMultiplayerDifficultyOverride`, `float? difficultyOverride`. There is **no** `GetNPCStatsScaleMultiplier` / `NPCStrengthOverride`; the override channel is `difficultyOverride` (a `GameDifficultyLevel` as `float`).

### `ScaleStats(int? activePlayersCount, float? strengthOverride)` — 18081–18105
```
if (NPCID.Sets.NeedsExpertScaling[type] || (lifeMax > 5 && damage != 0 && !friendly && !townNPC)) {
    difficulty = strengthOverride ?? Main.Difficulty;
    if (difficulty >= Expert && Main.hardMode) ScaleStats_ForExpertHardmode();   // 18088
    ScaleStats_ByDifficulty();                                                   // 18090
    if (difficulty >= Expert) ScaleStats_ByPlayerCount(activePlayersCount ?? GetActivePlayerCount()); // 18092
    if (!ProjectileNPC && lifeMax < 6) lifeMax = 6;
    life = lifeMax; defDamage = damage; defDefense = defense; defLifeMax = lifeMax;
}
```
`NPCID.Sets.NeedsExpertScaling` = `{25, 30, 665, 33, 112, 666, 261, 265, 371, 516, 519, 396, 397, 398, 491}` (`~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/Terarria1456/Terraria.ID/NPCID.cs:4799`) — projectile-like NPCs (Burning Sphere, Chaos Ball, Vile Spit, Moon Lord parts, Flying Dutchman, etc.).

### `ScaleStats_ByDifficulty()` — 18106–18115
```
if (!ProjectileNPC) { lifeMax *= GameDifficultyData.EnemyMaxLifeMultiplier.Sample(difficulty);
                      value   *= GameDifficultyData.EnemyMoneyDropMultiplier.Sample(difficulty); }
damage          = GetAttackDamage_ScaledByDifficulty(damage);
knockBackResist = GetKnockbackMultiplier_ScaledByDifficulty(knockBackResist);
ScaleStats_ByDifficulty_Tweaks();
```

### `ScaleStats_ByDifficulty_Tweaks()` — 18118–18415
Per-type `num` (damage mult), `num2` (life mult), `num3` (master life mult) with the same table used by `ScaleStats_ByPlayerCount`. Key entries: 210/211 Bee & Small Bee (×0.6 life, ×0.6 dmg), 5 EoC (0.75), 4 Demon Eye (0.65), 13–15 EoW (0.7), 266/267 Brain/Creeper (0.85), 50 King Slime (0.7), 471 Goblin Warlock (0.85), 472 Shadowflame Apparition (0.85/0.8), 222 Queen Bee (0.7), 113/114 WoF (0.7), 116 The Hungry (0.7), 117–119 Leech (0.8), 657 Queen Slime (0.8), 658–660 (0.75), 134–136 Destroyer (0.75), 139 Probe (0.75), 127–131 Skeletron Prime (0.75), 125/126 Twins (0.75), 262 Plantera (0.7), 264 Plantera's Tentacle (life only), 636 Empress (0.7), 245–249 Golem (0.75), 370 Fishron (0.65), 371–373 Fishron parts (0.75), 439/440/454–459/522/523 Cultist & dragon (0.75), 396–398 Moon Lord (0.75), 551 Betsy (0.75), OOA invaders (`BelongsToInvasionOldOnesArmy`, master reduction), invasion group −1/−2 generic (0.75 life, 0.65 for 315/325/327/344/345/346). Applied via
`lifeMax *= Remap(difficulty, Classic→Expert, 1→num2) * Remap(difficulty, Expert→Master, 1→num3)` (18373–18375) and `damage *= Remap(difficulty, Classic→Expert, 1→num)` (18377–18379).

### `ScaleStats_ForExpertHardmode()` — **18448–18496 (the 1.4.4 progression-based weak-NPC normalization)**
```
bool flag  = type >= 0 && NPCID.Sets.ProjectileNPC[type];
bool flag2 = !NPCID.Sets.DontDoHardmodeScaling[type];
// getGoodWorld exclusions (18453-18466): if (24|25)&&AnyNPCs(113), (32|33)&&AnyNPCs(35), 6&&AnyNPCs(13) → flag2 = false
if (boss || lifeMax >= 1000) flag2 = false;          // 18468-18471
if (!flag2) return;
int num = damage + defense + lifeMax / 4;            // 18476  (min 1)
int num2 = 80;                                       // 18481
if (downedPlantBoss) num2 += 20;                     // 18484  → 100
if (num < num2) {
    float num3 = num2 / num;
    damage = damage * num3 * 0.9f;
    if (!flag) {
        defense = defense * num3;
        lifeMax = lifeMax * num3 * 1.1;
        value   = value * num3 * 0.8;
    }
}
```
So: **Expert+Master hardmode only**, all non-boss NPCs with `damage + defense + lifeMax/4 < 80` (< 100 post-Plantera) are normalized **up** to that floor (damage ×0.9, life ×1.1, value ×0.8 of the raw ratio). `NPCID.Sets.DontDoHardmodeScaling` = `{5, 13, 14, 15, 267, 113, 114, 115, 116, 117, 118, 119, 658, 659, 660, 400, 522}` (`NPCID.cs:4440`) — Eater of Souls, EoW, Creeper, WoF + Hungry + Leech, Queen Slime's slimes, True Eye of Cthulhu, Ancient Light. This applies to **every** qualifying enemy (weak-NPC normalization), not a whitelist.

### `ScaleStats_ByPlayerCount(int numPlayers)` — 18498–18658
`statsAreScaledForThisManyPlayers = numPlayers; GetStatScalingFactors(numPlayers, out balance, out boost);` then per-type `lifeMax *= balance` for the same boss list as the Tweaks table (plus knockback-resist decay for multi-segment bosses when >4 players), and generic invasion group: `lifeMax *= 1.0 + (numPlayers-1)*0.2`.

### `GetStatScalingFactors(int numPlayers, out float balance, out float boost)` — **18660–18673**
```
balance = 1f; boost = 0.35f;
for (int i = 1; i < numPlayers; i++) { balance += boost; boost += (1f - boost) / 3f; }
if (balance > 8f)  balance = (balance * 2f + 8f) / 3f;
if (balance > 1000f) balance = 1000f;
```
Helpers: `GetBalance()` 18411 (uses `GetActivePlayerCount()`), `GetMyBalance()` 18418 (uses stored `statsAreScaledForThisManyPlayers`, returns 1 if ≤1), `GetActivePlayerCount()` 18434.

### Per-type `Main.hardMode` boosts in `SetDefaults` (8361–17794)
Only **four** occurrences in the entire 9,400-line `SetDefaults`:
- **9850–9855** (type **85 Mimic**): pre-hardmode override `damage = 30; defense = 12; lifeMax = 300; value = 2 gold` (hardmode keeps 80/30/500/10 platinum). Enables the pre-HM Mimic spawns at 3622/3633/3644/3671.
- **15942–15945** (type **548 Eternia Crystal**, friendly): `Main.hardMode && downedMechBossAny → defense 18, lifeMax 3000`.
- **15947–15950** (same): `downedGolemBoss && Main.hardMode → defense 20, lifeMax 5000`.
- **17034–17039** (type **629 Ice Mimic**): pre-hardmode `damage 30 / defense 12 / lifeMax 300 / 2 gold` vs hardmode 80/30/500.

No other `Main.hardMode`-conditional stats in `SetDefaults`.

## C. Spawn-rate / max-spawn changes by progression

All inside `Spawner.GetSpawnRate(Player, out int spawnRate, out int maxSpawns)` (383–802). `spawnRate` starts at `defaultSpawnRate`, `maxSpawns` at `defaultMaxSpawns`.

**The only progression gates are `Main.hardMode` and `downedBoss3`. There are no `downedMechBoss*`, `downedPlantBoss`, `downedGolemBoss`, `downedMoonlord` rate adjustments anywhere in the spawner.**

| Line | Condition | Effect |
|---|---|---|
| 387–391 | `Main.hardMode` (global) | `spawnRate = defaultSpawnRate * 0.9`, `maxSpawns = defaultMaxSpawns + 1` |
| 393 | player in Underworld | `maxSpawns *= 2` |
| 397–419 | below rock layer | non-remix: rate ×0.4, spawns ×1.9; remix: HM ×0.45/×1.8 vs pre-HM ×0.5/×1.7 |
| 421–433 | below world surface | remix ×0.4/×1.9; else HM ×0.45/×1.8 vs pre-HM ×0.5/×1.7 |
| 436–451 | night | ×0.6 / ×1.3; **`Main.bloodMoon` → ×0.3 / ×1.8**; Pumpkin/Frost moon at surface → ×0.2 / ×2 |
| 454 | day eclipse | rate ×0.2, spawns ×1.9 |
| 505–510 | `ZoneSandstorm` | **HM: rate ×0.4, spawns ×1.5 / pre-HM: rate ×0.9, spawns ×1.2** |
| 512–514 | underground desert | ×0.2 / ×3 |
| 540–542 | `ZoneLihzhardTemple` | ×0.8 / ×1.2 (remix ×0.4/×1.5) |
| 555–558 | Hallow below rock layer | ×0.65 / ×1.3 |
| 696 | `ZoneDungeon && !downedBoss3` | `spawnRate = 10` (Dungeon Guardian zone) |
| 660–663 | Pumpkin/Frost moon | `maxSpawns = defaultMaxSpawns*(2+0.3*players)`, `spawnRate = 20` |
| 666–669 | `invaders` | same override |
| 675–682 | Underworld + 1 town NPC | 50 % `noWorms`; 10 % `spawnFriendly`, else rate ×1.25 (scales to ×2 at 3 NPCs) |
| 712–730 | surface town NPC counts | 1 town NPC → `noWorms`, 1/3 friendly else rate ×2; 2 NPCs → 2/3 friendly else ×3; ≥3 → 1/10 noWorms-fail, 1/3 friendly else ×5 (graveyard variants 714/721) |
| 652–657, 668–673 | population-based | `nearbyActiveNPCs < maxSpawns*0.2/0.4/0.6/0.8` → rate ×0.6/0.7/0.8/0.9 |
| 647–650 | clamps | `spawnRate ≥ defaultSpawnRate*0.1`; `maxSpawns ≤ defaultMaxSpawns*3` |
| 654–656 | `Main.getGoodWorld` | ×0.8 / ×1.2 |

Other knobs (not progression): `player.invis` ×1.2/×0.8 (660s), `player.calmed` ×1.65/×0.6, sunflower ×1.2/×0.8, angler set ×1.3/×0.7, `player.enemySpawns` (battle potion) ×0.5/×2, water candle ×0.75/×1.5, peace candle ×1.3/×0.7, fairy ×1.2/×0.8, `Main.wofNPCIndex >= 0` in Underworld → spawns ×0.3 / rate ×3, `WorldGen.Skyblock.lowTiles` → rate /2.

Separate: `Spawner.SpawnNPC()` (185) also calls `SlimeRainSpawns` (5829–5888) during `Main.slimeRain` — no progression gates (expert multiplies the roll chance by 0.85).

---

## Caveats worth carrying into your implementation

1. **Line 1681** reads `ZoneCrimson` twice in a row (1677 spawns 242 Blood Jelly, 1681 spawns 241 Blood Feeder). One of these is almost certainly `ZoneCorrupt` upstream (241 Blood Feeder is the Corruption water enemy in vanilla); the decompile literally has `ZoneCrimson` in both. Treat as a decompiler fidelity issue and verify against another dump before porting.
2. The duplicate Martian-Probe branches at **1308/1312** and Wyvern branches at **1317/1321** differ only by a trailing `&& ZoneWaterCandle` — the second pair is unreachable as written.
3. `underGround` is inverted from intuition (it means *above* rock layer); `deeperThanRockLayer` is the true "cavern+" flag.
4. Post-Moon Lord changes **nothing** in natural spawning in 1.4.5.6 — if your fork wants ML-gated spawns, that is a non-vanilla extension.
````
